d(a, b) = sqrt((w * Δx)² + ((2 - w) * Δy)²)\n    def metric(u, v):\n        dx = u[0] - v[0]\n        dy = u[1] - v[1]\n        return np.sqrt((w * dx)**2 + ((2 - w) * dy)**2)\n\n    dist_condensed = pdist(points, metric=metric)\n    return squareform(dist_condensed)\n\n\ndef euclidean_distance(p1, p2):\n    \"\"\"Standard Euclidean distance for matching.\"\"\"\n    return np.sqrt((p1[0] - p2[0])**2 + (p1[1] - p2[1])**2)\n\n\ndef greedy_match(centroids, expert_pts, max_dist=100):\n    \"\"\"\n    Greedy matching: pair closest points first, up to max_dist.\n    Returns number of matches and list of distances for matched pairs.\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(centroids) == 0 or len(expert_pts) == 0:\n        return 0, []\n\n    # Compute all pairwise distances\n    distances = []\n    for i, c in enumerate(centroids):\n        for j, e in enumerate(expert_pts):\n            d = euclidean_distance(c, e)\n            if d <= max_dist:\n                distances.append((d, i, j))\n\n    # Sort by distance (closest first)\n    distances.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n\n    matched_centroids = set()\n    matched_experts = set()\n    match_distances = []\n\n    for d, i, j in distances:\n        if i not in matched_centroids and j not in matched_experts:\n            matched_centroids.add(i)\n            matched_experts.add(j)\n            match_distances.append(d)\n\n    return len(match_distances), match_distances\n\n\ndef evaluate_image(file_rad, min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Evaluate DBSCAN clustering for a single image.\n    Returns (F1, delta) where delta is NaN if no matches.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Get expert points for this image\n    if file_rad not in expert_grouped.groups:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    expert_pts = expert_grouped.get_group(file_rad)[['x', 'y']].values\n    n_expert = len(expert_pts)\n\n    # Get citizen science points for this image\n    if file_rad not in citsci_grouped.groups:\n        # No citizen science points => no clusters => F1 = 0\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    citsci_pts = citsci_grouped.get_group(file_rad)[['x', 'y']].values\n\n    if len(citsci_pts) == 0:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    # Run DBSCAN with custom metric\n    if len(citsci_pts) < min_samples:\n        # Not enough points to form any cluster\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    dist_matrix = custom_distance_matrix(citsci_pts, shape_weight)\n\n    db = DBSCAN(eps=epsilon, min_samples=min_samples, metric='precomputed')\n    labels = db.fit_predict(dist_matrix)\n\n    # Get cluster centroids (excluding noise, label = -1)\n    unique_labels = set(labels)\n    unique_labels.discard(-1)\n\n    if len(unique_labels) == 0:\n        # No clusters found\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    centroids = []\n    for label in unique_labels:\n        cluster_pts = citsci_pts[labels == label]\n        centroid = cluster_pts.mean(axis=0)\n        centroids.append(centroid)\n\n    centroids = np.array(centroids)\n    n_clusters = len(centroids)\n\n    # Greedy match centroids to expert points\n    n_matches, match_distances = greedy_match(centroids, expert_pts)\n\n    if n_matches == 0:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    # Compute F1\n    precision = n_matches / n_clusters if n_clusters > 0 else 0\n    recall = n_matches / n_expert if n_expert > 0 else 0\n\n    if precision + recall > 0:\n        f1 = 2 * precision * recall / (precision + recall)\n    else:\n        f1 = 0.0\n\n    # Delta is average distance of matched pairs\n    delta = np.mean(match_distances)\n\n    return f1, delta\n\n\ndef evaluate_params(min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Evaluate a single hyperparameter combination across all images.\n    Returns (avg_F1, avg_delta, min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight)\n    \"\"\"\n    f1_scores = []\n    deltas = []\n\n    for file_rad in expert_images:\n        f1, delta = evaluate_image(file_rad, min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight)\n        f1_scores.append(f1)\n        if not np.isnan(delta):\n            deltas.append(delta)\n\n    avg_f1 = np.mean(f1_scores)\n    avg_delta = np.mean(deltas) if deltas else np.nan\n\n    return avg_f1, avg_delta, min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight\n\n\ndef is_pareto_optimal(results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find Pareto-optimal points.\n    Objective: Maximize F1, Minimize delta\n    \"\"\"\n    n = len(results)\n    is_optimal = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)\n\n    for i in range(n):\n        for j in range(n):\n            if i == j:\n                continue\n            # j dominates i if j has >= F1 AND <= delta, with at least one strict\n            f1_i, delta_i = results[i][0], results[i][1]\n            f1_j, delta_j = results[j][0], results[j][1]\n\n            if f1_j >= f1_i and delta_j <= delta_i:\n                if f1_j > f1_i or delta_j < delta_i:\n                    is_optimal[i] = False\n                    break\n\n    return is_optimal\n\n\ndef main():\n    # Hyperparameter grid\n    min_samples_range = list(range(3, 10))  # 3-9\n    epsilon_range = list(range(4, 25, 2))   # 4, 6, 8, ..., 24\n    shape_weight_range = [round(0.9 + 0.1 * i, 1) for i in range(11)]  # 0.9-1.9\n\n    param_combinations = list(product(min_samples_range, epsilon_range, shape_weight_range))\n    print(f\"Total parameter combinations: {len(param_combinations)}\")\n\n    # Parallel evaluation\n    results = Parallel(n_jobs=-1, verbose=10)(\n        delayed(evaluate_params)(ms, eps, sw)\n        for ms, eps, sw in param_combinations\n    )\n\n    # Filter results: F1 > 0.5 and valid delta\n    filtered_results = [\n        r for r in results\n        if r[0] > 0.5 and not np.isnan(r[1])\n    ]\n\n    print(f\"Results with F1 > 0.5: {len(filtered_results)}\")\n\n    if len(filtered_results) == 0:\n        print(\"No results with F1 > 0.5 found!\")\n        # Write empty file\n        with open('/root/pareto_frontier.csv', 'w') as f:\n            f.write(\"F1,delta,min_samples,epsilon,shape_weight\\n\")\n        return\n\n    # Find Pareto frontier\n    is_optimal = is_pareto_optimal(filtered_results)\n    pareto_results = [r for r, opt in zip(filtered_results, is_optimal) if opt]\n\n    print(f\"Pareto-optimal points: {len(pareto_results)}\")\n\n    # Sort by F1 descending for output\n    pareto_results.sort(key=lambda x: -x[0])\n\n    # Write to CSV\n    with open('/root/pareto_frontier.csv', 'w') as f:\n        f.write(\"F1,delta,min_samples,epsilon,shape_weight\\n\")\n        for f1, delta, ms, eps, sw in pareto_results:\n            f.write(f\"{f1:.5f},{delta:.5f},{ms},{eps},{sw:.1f}\\n\")\n\n    print(\"Results written to /root/pareto_frontier.csv\")\n\n    # Print summary\n    for f1, delta, ms, eps, sw in pareto_results:\n        print(f\"F1={f1:.5f}, delta={delta:.5f}, min_samples={ms}, epsilon={eps}, shape_weight={sw:.1f}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":22057,"cache_read_input_tokens":43999,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":22057,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"82fde482-0036-4790-a619-8ee90f8e5e33","uuid":"d8a83bdb-6d7c-45db-860a-0ac2b7be44a5"}
